<section data-mw-section-id="2" id="mwHA" aria-labelledby="Location"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Location">Location</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Patratu&amp;action=edit&amp;section=2" title="Edit section: Location"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwHQ">Patratu is located at <span class="geo-inline" about="#mwt16" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwHg" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Coord","href":"./Template:Coord"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"23.67"},"2":{"wt":"N"},"3":{"wt":"85.28"},"4":{"wt":"E"},"5":{"wt":""}},"i":0}}]}'><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1156832818" about="#mwt17" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles" data-mw='{"name":"templatestyles","attrs":{"src":"Module:Coordinates/styles.css"},"body":{"extsrc":""}}'/><span class="plainlinks nourlexpansion"><a rel="mw:ExtLink" href="https://geohack.toolforge.org/geohack.php?pagename=Patratu&amp;params=23.67_N_85.28_E_" class="external text"><span class="geo-nondefault"><span class="geo-dms" title="Maps, aerial photos, and other data for this location"><span class="latitude">23°40′N</span> <span class="longitude">85°17′E</span></span></span><span class="geo-multi-punct"><span typeof="mw:Entity">﻿</span> / <span typeof="mw:Entity">﻿</span></span><span class="geo-default"><span class="geo-dec" title="Maps, aerial photos, and other data for this location">23.67°N 85.28°E</span><span style="display:none"><span typeof="mw:Entity">﻿</span> / <span class="geo">23.67; 85.28</span></span></span></a></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Pages_using_gadget_WikiMiniAtlas"/></span>.<sup about="#mwt18" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-1"}}'><a href="#cite_note-1" id="mwHw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwIA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwIQ">[</span>1<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwIg">]</span></span></a></sup> It has an average elevation of 405<span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mwIw"> </span>metres (1328<span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mwJA"> </span>feet).
Patratu Dam was constructed under the planning of the greatest Indian engineer and father of Indian engineering, Sir Mokshagundam Visvesvaraya.<sup about="#mwt19" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-2" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-2"}}'><a href="#cite_note-2" id="mwJQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwJg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwJw">[</span>2<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwKA">]</span></span></a></sup> This dam is only 40<span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mwKQ"> </span>km away from <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ranchi" title="Ranchi" id="mwKg">Ranchi</a>, the capital of Jharkhand, from where it can be reached by road in less than an hour. It is directly connected to <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ranchi" title="Ranchi" id="mwKw">Ranchi</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Patna" title="Patna" id="mwLA">Patna</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jaipur" title="Jaipur" id="mwLQ">Jaipur</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jamshedpur" title="Jamshedpur" id="mwLg">Jamshedpur</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Varanasi" title="Varanasi" id="mwLw">Varanasi</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jammu" title="Jammu" id="mwMA">Jammu</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jabalpur" title="Jabalpur" id="mwMQ">Jabalpur</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Allahabad" title="Allahabad" class="mw-redirect" id="mwMg">Allahabad</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Delhi" title="Delhi" id="mwMw">Delhi</a> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kolkata" title="Kolkata" id="mwNA">Kolkata</a> by train.</p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="3" id="mwNQ" aria-labelledby="Area_overview"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Area_overview">Area overview</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Patratu&amp;action=edit&amp;section=3" title="Edit section: Area overview"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwNg">Ramgarh has a vibrant coal-mining sector. The map alongside provides links to five operational areas of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Central_Coalfields" title="Central Coalfields" id="mwNw">Central Coalfields</a> spread across <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/South_Karanpura_Coalfield" title="South Karanpura Coalfield" id="mwOA">South Karanpura Coalfield</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ramgarh_Coalfield" title="Ramgarh Coalfield" id="mwOQ">Ramgarh Coalfield</a> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/West_Bokaro_Coalfield" title="West Bokaro Coalfield" id="mwOg">West Bokaro Coalfield</a>. Four of the six CD blocks in the district have coal mines – Patratu, Ramgarh, Mandu and Chitarpur. The high concentration of census towns in these blocks are noticeable on the map. Only two blocks, Gola and Dulmi, are totally rural areas. Ramgarh district lies in the central part of the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chota_Nagpur_Plateau" title="Chota Nagpur Plateau" id="mwOw">Chota Nagpur Plateau</a>. The <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Damodar_River" title="Damodar River" id="mwPA">Damodar valley</a> covers most of the district. The forested areas in highlands to the north and the south of the valley can be seen in the map (mark the shaded areas).<sup about="#mwt22" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-3" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-3"}}'><a href="#cite_note-3" id="mwPQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwPg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwPw">[</span>3<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwQA">]</span></span></a></sup> "Chotanagpur has a charm of its own... The entire area forms one of the most charming series of views imaginable. The far-off hills in the background in exquisite tints of blue or purple as the light falls, the nearer hills picturesquely shaped and luxuriant in every shade of green with their bold escarpments in black or grey, and the brown plains below furnishing their quota of colours."<sup about="#mwt25" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-4" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-4"}}'><a href="#cite_note-4" id="mwQQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwQg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwQw">[</span>4<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwRA">]</span></span></a></sup></p> 

<p id="mwRQ"><small id="mwRg">Note: The map alongside presents some of the notable locations in the district. All places marked in the map are linked in the larger full screen map.</small></p>

</section></section><section data-mw-section-id="4" id="mwRw" aria-labelledby="Civic_administration"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Civic_administration">Civic administration</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Patratu&amp;action=edit&amp;section=4" title="Edit section: Civic administration"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<section data-mw-section-id="5" id="mwSA" aria-labelledby="Police_station"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Police_station">Police station</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Patratu&amp;action=edit&amp;section=5" title="Edit section: Police station"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwSQ">Patratu police station serves Patratu CD block.<sup about="#mwt28" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-5" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-5"}}'><a href="#cite_note-5" id="mwSg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwSw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwTA">[</span>5<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwTQ">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="6" id="mwTg" aria-labelledby="CD_block_HQ"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="CD_block_HQ">CD block HQ</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Patratu&amp;action=edit&amp;section=6" title="Edit section: CD block HQ"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwTw">The headquarters of Patratu CD block is located at Patratu town.<sup about="#mwt31" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-6" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-6"}}'><a href="#cite_note-6" id="mwUA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwUQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwUg">[</span>6<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwUw">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section></section><section data-mw-section-id="7" id="mwVA" aria-labelledby="Demographics"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Demographics">Demographics</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Patratu&amp;action=edit&amp;section=7" title="Edit section: Demographics"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwVQ">According to the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2011_Census_of_India" title="2011 Census of India" class="mw-redirect" id="mwVg">2011 Census of India</a>, Patratu had a total population of 32,899, of which 17,305 (53%) were males and 15,594 (47%) were females. Population in the age range 0-6 years was 3,999 (12%). The total number of literate persons in Patratu was 24,382 (84.37% of the population over 6 years).<sup about="#mwt34" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-7" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-7"}}'><a href="#cite_note-7" id="mwVw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwWA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwWQ">[</span>7<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwWg">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwWw">As of the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2001_Census_of_India" title="2001 Census of India" class="mw-redirect" id="mwXA">2001 Census of India</a>,<sup about="#mwt37" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-census_2001_8-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"census 2001"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-census_2001-8"}}'><a href="#cite_note-census_2001-8" id="mwXQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwXg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwXw">[</span>8<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwYA">]</span></span></a></sup> Patratu had a population of 32,132, of which 17,311 (54%) were male and 14,821 (46%) were female. Population in the age range 0-6 was 4,198 (13%). In 2001, Patratu had an average literacy rate of 70%, higher than the national average of 59.5%: male literacy was 79%, and female literacy was 61%.<sup about="#mwt38" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-census_2001_8-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"census 2001"}}'><a href="#cite_note-census_2001-8" id="mwYQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwYg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwYw">[</span>8<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwZA">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="8" id="mwZQ" aria-labelledby="Infrastructure"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Infrastructure">Infrastructure</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Patratu&amp;action=edit&amp;section=8" title="Edit section: Infrastructure"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwZg">According to the <i id="mwZw">District Census Handbook 2011, Ramgarh</i>, Patratu covered an area of 1.392<span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mwaA"> </span>km<sup id="mwaQ">2</sup>. Among the civic amenities, it had 25<span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mwag"> </span>km roads with both open and closed drains, the protected water supply involved tap water from treated sources, uncovered wells, overhead tanks. It had 5,822 domestic electric connections, 120 road lighting points. Among the medical facilities, it had 4 hospitals, 1 dispensary, 1 health centre, 1 family welfare centre, 8 maternity and child welfare centres, 6 maternity homes, 16 nursing homes, 10 medicine shops. Among the educational facilities it had 14 primary schools, 6 middle schools, 5 secondary schools, 2 senior secondary schools, 1 general degree college. It had 1 non-formal educational centre (Sarva Siksha Abhiyan). Among the social, recreational and cultural facilities it had 1 stadium, 1 cinema theatre, 3 auditorium/ community halls, 3 public libraries, 1 reading room. An important commodity It produced was electricity. It had the branch offices of 3 nationalised banks, 1 agricultural credit society.<sup about="#mwt41" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-9" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-9"}}'><a href="#cite_note-9" id="mwaw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwbA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwbQ">[</span>9<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwbg">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="9" id="mwbw" aria-labelledby="Education"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Education">Education</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Patratu&amp;action=edit&amp;section=9" title="Edit section: Education"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwcA">Schools in Patratu include:</p>
<ul id="mwcQ"><li id="mwcg">S.S High School was established on 1 January 1963. It is located beside road leading to Patratu dam and subsequently Ranchi. It is a 10+2 school with a large area and a big playground.</li>
<li id="mwcw">Kendriya Vidyalaya Patratu</li>
<li id="mwdA">D.A.V. Public School Patratu</li>
<li id="mwdQ">O P Jindal School Patratu</li>
<li id="mwdg">Patratu School of Economics</li>
<li id="mwdw">Saraswati Shishu Vidya Mandir, Patratu</li>
<li id="mweA">Jawahar Navodaya Vidyalaya, Patratu</li></ul>

<ul class="gallery mw-gallery-traditional" typeof="mw:Extension/gallery" about="#mwt42" id="mweQ" data-mw='{"name":"gallery","attrs":{},"body":{}}'>
<li class="gallerybox" style="width: 155px;" id="mweg"><div class="thumb" style="width: 150px; height: 150px;" id="mwew"><span typeof="mw:File" id="mwfA"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:S.s_High_School_Patratu.jpg" class="mw-file-description" title="S.S. High School 2" id="mwfQ"><img resource="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:S.s_High_School_Patratu.jpg" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/b/b5/S.s_High_School_Patratu.jpg/120px-S.s_High_School_Patratu.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail" decoding="async" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/b/b5/S.s_High_School_Patratu.jpg/250px-S.s_High_School_Patratu.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail 2x" alt="S.S. High School 2" data-file-width="2816" data-file-height="2112" data-file-type="bitmap" height="90" width="120" class="mw-file-element" id="mwfg"/></a></span></div><div class="gallerytext" id="mwfw">S.S. High School 2</div></li>
</ul>

<section data-mw-section-id="10" id="mwgA" aria-labelledby="Colleges"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Colleges">Colleges</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Patratu&amp;action=edit&amp;section=10" title="Edit section: Colleges"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwgQ">Patratu Thermal Power Station (PTPS) College, affiliated with <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vinoba_Bhave_University" title="Vinoba Bhave University" id="mwgg">Vinoba Bhave University</a> was established at Patratu in 1972.<sup about="#mwt45" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-10" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-10"}}'><a href="#cite_note-10" id="mwgw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwhA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwhQ">[</span>10<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwhg">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section></section><section data-mw-section-id="11" id="mwhw" aria-labelledby="Transportation"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Transportation">Transportation</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Patratu&amp;action=edit&amp;section=11" title="Edit section: Transportation"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<section data-mw-section-id="12" id="mwiA" aria-labelledby="Airways"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Airways">Airways</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Patratu&amp;action=edit&amp;section=12" title="Edit section: Airways"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwiQ"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Birsa_Munda_Airport" title="Birsa Munda Airport" id="mwig">Birsa Munda Airport</a> is the nearest airport, with direct flights available to <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Delhi" title="Delhi" id="mwiw">Delhi</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kolkata" title="Kolkata" id="mwjA">Kolkata</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mumbai" title="Mumbai" id="mwjQ">Mumbai</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hyderabad" title="Hyderabad" id="mwjg">Hyderabad</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jharsuguda" title="Jharsuguda" id="mwjw">Jharsuguda</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Indore" title="Indore" id="mwkA">Indore</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bhubaneswar" title="Bhubaneswar" id="mwkQ">Bhubaneswar</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bangalore" title="Bangalore" class="mw-redirect" id="mwkg">Bangalore</a> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Patna" title="Patna" id="mwkw">Patna</a>.</p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="13" id="mwlA" aria-labelledby="Railways"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Railways">Railways</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Patratu&amp;action=edit&amp;section=13" title="Edit section: Railways"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwlQ"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Patratu_Railway_Station?action=edit&amp;redlink=1" title="Patratu Railway Station (page does not exist)" class="new" typeof="mw:LocalizedAttrs" id="mwlg" data-mw-i18n='{"title":{"lang":"x-page","key":"red-link-title","params":["Patratu Railway Station"]}}'>Patratu Railway Station</a> is the only railway station. It has three platforms with direct trains available for Kolkata in Up direction, and in the down direction <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/New_Delhi" title="New Delhi" id="mwlw">New Delhi</a>, Patna and many states of India like <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Punjab,_India" title="Punjab, India" id="mwmA">Punjab</a> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Haryana" title="Haryana" id="mwmQ">Haryana</a>.</p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="14" id="mwmg" aria-labelledby="Roadways"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Roadways">Roadways</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Patratu&amp;action=edit&amp;section=14" title="Edit section: Roadways"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwmw">Patratu is connected by <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/State_Highway_2_(Jharkhand)" title="State Highway 2 (Jharkhand)" id="mwnA">State Highway 2</a> of Jharkhand, to its district headquarters at <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ramgarh_district" title="Ramgarh district" id="mwnQ">Ramgarh</a> and state capital at <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ranchi" title="Ranchi" id="mwng">Ranchi</a>. There is also a bus station where buses are available for many cities of Jharkhand and West Bengal.</p>

</section></section><section data-mw-section-id="15" id="mwnw" aria-labelledby="Latest_happenings"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Latest_happenings">Latest happenings</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Patratu&amp;action=edit&amp;section=15" title="Edit section: Latest happenings"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwoA"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jharkhand" title="Jharkhand" id="mwoQ">Jharkhand</a> <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chief_Minister" title="Chief Minister" class="mw-redirect" id="mwog">Chief Minister</a> <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Raghubar_Das" title="Raghubar Das" id="mwow">Raghubar Das</a> on 13 July 2015 inaugurated the Patratu unit of Burnpur Cement.<sup about="#mwt48" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-11" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-11"}}'><a href="#cite_note-11" id="mwpA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwpQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwpg">[</span>11<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwpw">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="16" id="mwqA" aria-labelledby="Patratu_Dam"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Patratu_Dam">Patratu Dam</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Patratu&amp;action=edit&amp;section=16" title="Edit section: Patratu Dam"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwqQ">The dam was made to store water of the Nalkari River for supply to the Ramgarh cantonment area as well as being the main reservoir for the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Patratu_Thermal_Power_Station" title="Patratu Thermal Power Station" id="mwqg">Patratu Thermal Power Station</a> (PTPS). There have been plans to turn it into a tourist attraction<sup about="#mwt51" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-12" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-12"}}'><a href="#cite_note-12" id="mwqw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwrA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwrQ">[</span>12<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwrg">]</span></span></a></sup></p>
<ul class="gallery mw-gallery-traditional" typeof="mw:Extension/gallery" about="#mwt52" id="mwrw" data-mw='{"name":"gallery","attrs":{"widths":"200px","heights":"150px"},"body":{}}'>
<li class="gallerybox" style="width: 235px;" id="mwsA"><div class="thumb" style="width: 230px; height: 180px;" id="mwsQ"><span typeof="mw:File" id="mwsg"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Nalkari_River.jpg" class="mw-file-description" title="View of Nalkari River" id="mwsw"><img resource="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Nalkari_River.jpg" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/e/e3/Nalkari_River.jpg/250px-Nalkari_River.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail" decoding="async" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/e/e3/Nalkari_River.jpg/500px-Nalkari_River.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail 2x" alt="View of Nalkari River" data-file-width="1824" data-file-height="1368" data-file-type="bitmap" height="150" width="200" class="mw-file-element" id="mwtA"/></a></span></div><div class="gallerytext" id="mwtQ">View of Nalkari River</div></li>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="17" id="mwwg" aria-labelledby="Patratu_valley"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Patratu_valley">Patratu valley</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Patratu&amp;action=edit&amp;section=17" title="Edit section: Patratu valley"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<figure class="mw-default-size" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" id="mwww"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Patratu_valley.jpg" class="mw-file-description" id="mwxA"><img resource="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Patratu_valley.jpg" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/1/15/Patratu_valley.jpg/250px-Patratu_valley.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail" decoding="async" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/1/15/Patratu_valley.jpg/500px-Patratu_valley.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail 2x" data-file-width="4608" data-file-height="3456" data-file-type="bitmap" height="188" width="250" class="mw-file-element" id="mwxQ"/></a><figcaption id="mwxg">View of Patratu valley</figcaption></figure>
<p id="mwxw">During recent changes, the area's coal pulling bicycles have gotten the support of local motor bikers on a profit sharing basis.<sup about="#mwt55" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-13" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-13"}}'><a href="#cite_note-13" id="mwyA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwyQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwyg">[</span>13<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwyw">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="18" id="mwzA" aria-labelledby="Patratu_Thermal_Power_Station"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Patratu_Thermal_Power_Station">Patratu Thermal Power Station</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Patratu&amp;action=edit&amp;section=18" title="Edit section: Patratu Thermal Power Station"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>

<p id="mwzQ">The attraction of this city is its natural habitat and scenic beauty. The city is developed as a residential area for employees of the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Patratu_Thermal_Power_Station" title="Patratu Thermal Power Station" id="mwzg">Patratu Thermal Power Station</a>. This <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Thermal_power_station" title="Thermal power station" id="mwzw">thermal power station</a> was made by Russian collaboration in 1962. It has two chimneys made by a Russian firm, which are called the Russian side. The other two chimneys were made by an Indian firm, so it is called the Indian side. The plant has a power generation capacity of 840<span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mw0A"> </span>MW.<sup about="#mwt58" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-14" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-14"}}'><a href="#cite_note-14" id="mw0Q"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mw0g"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mw0w">[</span>14<span class="cite-bracket" id="mw1A">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt62" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-15" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-15"}}'><a href="#cite_note-15" id="mw1Q"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mw1g"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mw1w">[</span>15<span class="cite-bracket" id="mw2A">]</span></span></a></sup>  Construction of 4000<span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mw2Q"> </span>MW power plant is going on in Patratu, the first unit will start in 2024.<sup about="#mwt65" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-16" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-16"}}'><a href="#cite_note-16" id="mw2g"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mw2w"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mw3A">[</span>16<span class="cite-bracket" id="mw3Q">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="19" id="mw3g" aria-labelledby="References"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="References">References</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Patratu&amp;action=edit&amp;section=19" title="Edit section: References"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
